# SOH — Khôi phục nguồn và đối chiếu 214 năng lực W00 → Bible → Source → Recipe → Action → Code → Test → Trải nghiệm

**Ngày khóa nguồn:** 2026-11-05. **TRẠNG THÁI:** SOURCE_RECONSTRUCTION_PARTIAL; NO GAMEPLAY PRODUCT ACCEPTANCE; NO DISPATCH; NO MERGE.

- Gameplay PR [#44](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/pull/44) `b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9`: OPEN/DRAFT/HOLD. No latest-head check runs independently found in the earlier audit.
- Map pilot `b12d05866366de0d50e4d305ff7b72fcf36b4921`: [LW-01 handoff](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b12d05866366de0d50e4d305ff7b72fcf36b4921/large-world-01/docs/CONTROL_TOWER_HANDOFF.md) **U=1 2000×1400**, old Gameplay copy `e11a9b44`, not V17 production.
- AVFX `6be8418273acb8c61a12aabac309637fbef04aa6`: 20 ARCANA visuals ≠ 7 Creator Core × 3 abilities.
- Recovered category ledger: [W00 214 rows](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/docs/survival/gameplay-depth/CAPABILITY_LEDGER_W00.md), **agent-inventoried, not proven equal to Creator original 214**.
- Cross-reference history: [108 Atlas entries](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/arena/dea2428e-soul-of-hero/docs/survival/SOURCE_COVERAGE_TRACE.json) = 44 historic SYS + 21 POI + 31 DEP + 12 journeys. Not a 1:1 mapping to SYS01–SYS18.
- [Original Atlas](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/a8c718b55130cff29b3adc5e7c531f9211ca22df/docs/survival/source_archive/Soul_of_Heroes_Atlas_Last_Day_Rules_Research_2026.md) is period/mode-specific; no direct transplant of Last Island stats.
- [Only operative Bible](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/ref/bible.md) contains K1–K8 and C01–C24; C source links in table are **candidate contract mappings at system level**, not verified one-to-one references.
- 5 source ZIPs v7–v11 and `SOH_MASTER_ACCEPTANCE_PROTOCOL_CANON_2026-11-05.md` not recovered. Approx 104-edge/other detailed acceptance counts from older handoff **not restored**.
- W00 historical status distribution: **PARTIAL 147, MISSING 60, OWNER_BLOCKED 5, PROPOSED_NOT_CREATOR_APPROVED 2**. They are **historical W00 labels**, NOT current release verdicts. Added V17/loot-respawn corrections are noted per affected row.
- Each row links a genuine *historical analog*, Bible family, producer/recipe/action trace proposal, real source symbol pointer and relevant category test **without claiming that those tests prove that row**. Generic rows are OPEN TRACE, not individual implementation verification.
- No runtime browser, media, blind-human or 336h run was executed in this audit. **All 214 rows remain NOT PRODUCT ACCEPTED.**

## 2. 214 hàng — ma trận chuỗi 8 lớp (đánh dấu đúng độ sâu nguồn)

**Cách đọc:** (1) Nghiên cứu = historical analog URL, không chứng nhận cùng cơ chế SOH. (2) Bible C = nhóm hợp đồng cần kiểm. (3) Producer = nguồn vật lý/phạm vi xác minh. (4) Recipe = công thức hoặc yêu cầu recipe. (5) Action = hành vi cụ thể. (6) Code = điểm đọc code, **không tương đương code pass**. (7) Test = file test liên quan, **không tương đương test per-row pass**. (8) Trải nghiệm/acceptance = việc phải xem/đo. Cột `W00→V17 note` phân biệt trạng thái cũ và bằng chứng mới.

| ID | Năng lực | Nghiên cứu LI (analog) | Bible | Nguồn vật lý | Recipe / quyền | Action | Code candidate | Test candidate | W00→V17 evidence caveat | Player-facing acceptance gate |

## 3. Sáu chuỗi chi tiết đã đối chiếu sâu hơn hàng mục lục

### A. Knife-first → tìm nguyên liệu → tool → sử dụng

- **Nghiên cứu:** LI `SYS-11, SYS-34, SYS-38, DEP-02`; áp dụng cơ chế cơ hội dùng ngay/khai thác, không bắt Core hay boss.
- **Bible:** C01/C03/C05/C24. **Producer:** `SOURCES.oak` HP18 stock6 wood1/hit; `rock` HP18 stock6 stone1/hit; dao khởi đầu.
- **Recipe:** `stone_pick` = wood3 + stone4 + rope1, crafting 2s. **Action:** `harvestHit`, `craft`, attack tool-based.
- **Code:** [catalog sources](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/catalog.ts#L141-L163), [recipes](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/catalog.ts#L193), [engine harvesting](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L423).
- **Test/experience:** historical [Salt chain checkpoint](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b12d05866366de0d50e4d305ff7b72fcf36b4921/large-world-01/web-large/IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKPOINT.md) documents ordinary keyboard/mouse journey, but copied old baseline, not V17 WMP. Natural player on current Veyra multiple spawns **NOT_TESTED**. Verdict **PARTIAL / PLAYABILITY_NOT_ACCEPTED**.

### B. Car Wreck oil → nghiên cứu → Fuel Can → lighthouse power

- **Nghiên cứu:** [POI-01 + DEP-09](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/a8c718b55130cff29b3adc5e7c531f9211ca22df/docs/survival/source_archive/Soul_of_Heroes_Atlas_Last_Day_Rules_Research_2026.md#L922-L927); only supply-demand principle carries over.
- **Bible:** C02/C06/C13; fuel must have real producer, consumption and alternate sinks.
- **Producer:** [wreck economy](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/wreck-economy.ts) + finite `wreck` source [catalog](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/catalog.ts#L141). V17 describes 35% `salvaged_oil` bonus per 8-stock wreck; **SOH-tuned value, not LI_VERIFIED**.
- **Recipe:** [`refine_salvaged_oil`](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/catalog.ts#L193) = 3 salvaged_oil + 1 glass → Fuel Can, furnace 18s plus burning fuel; V17 states 60 power-sec per can.
- **Code:** [harvestHit](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L423), [craft](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L785), [generator](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L866).
- **Test:** [v17-oil-chain](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/tests/v17-oil-chain.test.ts) explicitly clears creatures, teleports to each wreck, injects glass. It does **not** prove natural industrial extraction, refinery, vehicles, or WMP reachability.
- **Verdict:** **SCOPED_CHAIN_TEST_EXISTS / SYS10 NOT ACCEPTED**.

### C. Home Garden Tender → offline → return

- **Nghiên cứu:** LI DEP-15/DEP-29: independent inputs, processing time, homecoming with benefit.
- **Bible:** C06+C09+C14, real-time both online/offline; farm funded/owned.
- **Producer:** seed/water/soil/tender/barrel/chest and fuel; recipe consumers `root_broth`/`herb_roast` and travel supply.
- **Code:** [gardenEnvironment](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L1155), [stepTenders](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L1202).
- **Test:** [home-production](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/tests/home-production.test.ts) contains chronology/offline/water/rain/out-of-stock tests but sets up machine and injects supplies, and clears creatures.
- **UX:** actual natural crops after real absence and season/weather growth, first-time discoverability, successful harvest-to-meal and player joy **NOT_OBSERVED**.
- **Verdict:** **FOCUSED TECHNICAL EVIDENCE / GAME FEEL OPEN**.

### D. Core discovery → recipe/energy → LMB/Q/E → world effect

- **Nghiên cứu:** LI rare-found-use-or-study; a behavioral analogy, Gold Core is SOH design.
- **Bible:** C03/C05/C19. **Source:** 7 Creator Core body sources only in Shipping/Dev crates [creator-loot](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/creator-loot.ts); [v17 contract test](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/tests/v17-core-contract.test.ts) **asserts zero WMP authoring sites**.
- **Recipe:** [catalog](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/catalog.ts#L193) includes Basic8 (stone2 + fiber2), Refined4 (crystal1 + iron_ingot1), 4 elemental essences, and seven body recipes; 40/12/4 load capacities are [contract](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/creator-contract.ts).
- **Action:** input/aim/loadCore, LMB/Q/E, real targets, interrupt, spend, UID/save/reuse. [7×17 matrix](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/docs/survival/gameplay-depth/V16_CORE_CAPABILITY_MATRIX.md) has no fully accepted row.
- **Visual:** owner 20 ARCANA effects are not equal to the 21 Creator actions, and no 21-phase Gold visual acceptance. **Verdict:** TECHNICAL PARTIAL / WORLD SOURCE BLOCKED / VISUAL NOT ACCEPTED.

### E. Final72 Ragnarok Cash → buy chest → physical home → deposit → reset

- **Research:** historical DEP-14/DEP-18 adapted; old badge/meta and permanent base are REJECTED. **Bible:** C15–C18/C23.
- **Producer:** unique final72 POI chest with cash; player travels to real seller and buys run-local escrow chest using unsecured cash; builds at lawful owned home and banks cash exactly once.
- **Action:** [openCash](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L874), [buy](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L901), [deposit](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L881), [endRun](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/src/game/engine.ts#L486).
- **Test:** [escrow-route-physical](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9/web/tests/escrow-route-physical.test.ts): controlled shift `r.qaOffset=264*HOUR`, clear monsters, inject wood10/stone5. The physical route results in **36 cash → 30 chest → 6 secured**. Route collision is real but phase/resources are assisted.
- **Verdict:** **ASSISTED E2 / no natural 336h first-run proof**; release-critical.

### F. WMP-04R1 U=1 World → actual gameplay sources

- **Research:** LI SYS-02/03/05 and DEP-10/23; Bible C13/C24, no arbitrary map quota.
- **Map:** [LW-01](https://github.com/Khanh-glitch/Soul-of-Hero/blob/b12d05866366de0d50e4d305ff7b72fcf36b4921/large-world-01/docs/CONTROL_TOWER_HANDOFF.md) tests chunk streaming in 2,000×1,400 authored extent, all 3 crossings + return, bounded residency; 2,000×2,000 capacity fixture is **NON-CANON**.
- **Blocking edge:** pilot copies older Gameplay `e11a9b44`, `H/J` harvest/build are QA only, no producer/POI/customer full V17 loop; helicopter missing; final world scale not approved. WMP latest gameplay scenes do not supply seven Core crates.
- **Required proof:** rebase/adapt against exact `b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9`, migrate saves/source IDs, physically reachable legal nodes/vendor/gates/POIs, natural 4-spawn trials at 640/1280 with no teleports or injection. **Verdict:** PERFORMANCE/STREAMING PILOT ≠ PRODUCTION INTEGRATION.

## 4. What can be decided without inventing approval

1. **No acceptance/merge**: `b5c8004ca4e77be62484fb3cd296fe296d7de4c9` remains unverified at natural, media, human and 336h gates; PR44 DRAFT/HOLD.
2. **No Arena dispatch yet**: owner current RUNNING/IDLE is not visible in GitHub; overlapping prompts prohibited.
3. **Cross-owner information request (not an implementation command):** Map deliver current WMP U=1 integration *adapted to V17 Gameplay* and authored source/POI/evac/Core eligibility; AVFX provide owner 21-action phase mapping and source asset custody; Gameplay produce natural journey evidence for cash, home, Core and fuel.
4. **Absent sources:** original master 5280-line file + v7–v11 packages still unavailable; this is a **trace skeleton with honest evidence gaps**, not reconstruction of all original detailed edge/gate enumerations.
5. **Next gate before command:** pin common composition; prove one first-spawn no-inject long chain `knife→loot→learn→craft→home→off→return→Ragnarok no boss→buy/deposit→wipe` and screen capture. Then produce owner-specific corrective one-shot orders.

## 5. Handoff

**Owner:** independent Gameplay Control Tower. **Evidence level:** textual current source + specific tests reviewed, no new run/captures. **Merge:** HOLD. **Arena agents dispatched:** NO. **Audit branch:** `audit/20261105-gameplay-source-to-experience` (documentation only), no source gameplay changed. **Next:** reconcile all 214 provisional rows to missing original Creator enumeration if recovered; drill individual rows that have only category pointers; target full normal-input browser source→sink journeys, matching WMP+AVFX exact SHA; then issue a verdict or owner order.
